Claude found that a refused change left the role vacant: its old binding was kept, but still counted as waiting for its checkpoint, so nothing dispatched it. Design correction (ADR-022 decision 8, third version, the refused change only): availability is judged before any live attempt; a change whose new agent is unavailable is refused and the old binding, its epoch and session go on being dispatched; the refusal is judged again at every checkpoint, and once the agent is there the role is held from dispatch until its attempts end, then the change is applied; each turn of the verdict is one event (`team.binding_refused`, `team.binding_pending`), and whether a role waits is the last verdict of its (run, role, revision), none meaning it waits; a later revision is judged afresh; the view shows `pending.refused`. Decision 10 now says the tick looks at the agents of the project's team before the checkpoint (S6), that the binder looks again each tick at the bindings it blocked, and that missing authentication is seen only by a turn: refusing a handoff for it is M2-T17's (REQ-019 failure mode 3), fallback M2-T15's.

Test: a missing apply_at, `{apply_at}` alone and `[]` are INVALID_TEAM_CONFIG with `apply_at`, `payload`, `payload` and current_revision 1; nothing written. |

Design corrections (ADR-022): decision 12, an adapter's terminal state is kept whole (UNKNOWN_EFFECT ends the attempt UNKNOWN_EFFECT with its work effect left STARTED, its task untouched, nothing run again); decision 10, the registry looks at every binding's agent, the checkpoint is given the agents found available and unavailable (with why) so any other is unknown whatever team it reads, the executor takes a work only once its agent was looked at, and the host's stop ends the probe running (ProbeEnv.cancel) and waits for it; decision 13, the gate resets every catchable signal and clears its mask before exec (signal-hook handlers, nix 0.31.3's pthread_sigmask: the workspace forbids unsafe); decision 17, the configured cancel graces reach every step of the host's ladder.

The unit check's other failure, `probe::tests::a_reading_given_up_stops_and_lets_everything_go` (the follow-up from M2-T06 round 6), was reproduced under a 14-process CPU load (6 of 12) and its cause found: a joined thread's task leaves /proc/self/task just after the join returns (the join wakes on the tid futex, cleared before the task is released; seen as a running `hoh-schema` task). The test now waits, within 1 s, for the task count after a join; under the same load, 0 of 16 failed afterwards.

Design correction (ADR-022 decision 10 v4; -300 is -202's class a second time): the probe's commands are ended by whoever stops, not by the thread that started them. Each command is registered with the registry's stop (`ProbeStop`) as it starts, the stop check, the spawn and the registration one step under its lock; `ProbeStop::stop` kills each registered command whose leader is not reaped (its group and its leader) before it returns; the thread that started it still reaps it, taking it out first, so no reaped number is signalled. A stopped schema reading is given its cleanup grace before it is given up, so its output is removed. A look the stop ended is not recorded (-352). Decision 12 gains the cancel's precedence over an unknown effect (-301); decisions 6, 9 and 17 the lows.

| ID | Severity | Found by | Summary | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T07R-300 | high | Codex | `codex_schema` returned on a stop without joining its reader; with the `hoh-schema` thread delayed (ptrace, 100 and 250 ms) the host exited first and the schema command was orphaned (PPid 1). | Decision 10 v4 above. Tests: in `hoh-agents`, a command whose probe stalls just after starting it is killed with its group by the stop alone, and a stopped probe starts nothing (red with the stop only setting its flag: both alive); a schema reading stopped while generating returns its own end with its output removed (red without the grace: the output left); in the product, a real `hoh __host` stopped by SIGTERM while its `hoh-schema` thread is held by ptrace: the schema command and its child are dead once the host exited, exit 0 (red with the stop only setting its flag: both alive). |
| M2T07R-301 | medium | Codex | An unknown effect with a committed CancelRun ended CANCELLED with the effect STARTED by the runner, but UNKNOWN_EFFECT/UNKNOWN by a reconcile (its cancel precedence covered only FAILED and SETTLED). | Decision 12 (ADR-021 decision 8e): both CANCELLED, the effect UNKNOWN and named in a diagnostic, the task untouched, nothing counted or retried. Test: both paths, then the cancel's last transaction (red before: STARTED by the runner; UNKNOWN_EFFECT by the reconcile). |
| M2T07R-350 | low | Claude | `HostConfig::product`'s native grace for a lease-expiry settle was untested. | Test: the product's host takes 3/4 s for CancelRun's ladder and the settle (red when the line is removed). |
| M2T07R-351 | low | Claude | `Unsynced::Moving` (three tries overtaken: 409 STALE_REVISION, no run) was untested. | Deferred: nothing in the store can make every try stale deterministically (its writes are IMMEDIATE transactions, and the team's revision is checked before anything is written, so neither a trigger nor a fault hook can move it in between); the branch is three lines read in the review. |
| M2T07R-352 | low | Claude | A look the host's stop ended was cached as the agent unavailable; a dispatch waiting for the first look could block its task BLOCKED_CAPABILITY. | Decision 10 v4: not recorded. Test: a look ended by the stop leaves the agent not looked at (red before: unavailable, "cancelled: whoever probes is stopping"). |
| M2T07R-353 | low | Claude | A settle waited the native grace even when it sent no native cancel. | Decision 17: the native grace only after a native cancel, else the SIGTERM grace. Test of the rule (red before). |
| M2T07R-354 | low | Claude | Decision 9's fresh native session for a session that cannot resume does not hold for Pi. | Decision 9: a usable Pi always offers resume (its interface needs `--session-id`), so the rule matters for Claude and Codex; Pi follows ADR-013 decision 1. |
| M2T07R-355 | low | Claude | Validated keys no product code reads had no owner; `project.default_mode` (ADR-014 gives it to M2-T07) was still the TUI's constant. | The TUI's top bar shows the configured mode (AUTO without a file or with an invalid one; test, red before); decision 6 names the task of every other key (and corrects ADR-020 decision 10: protocol repairs come with M2-T16). |

Design corrections (ADR-022): decision 10 v5 (-450 is the probe-at-stop class a third time): the stop never waits for a spawn: a start registers an empty slot under the stop's lock (none once stopped) and spawns outside it; once spawned, its starter puts the child in its slot and then looks at the stop, while the stop sets its flag and then looks at each slot, so one of them kills it whichever comes second; the residual (an exec that never returns before the host exits, its pid known to no one) is stated. Decision 12 v3 (-451 is the -301 class a second time): one rule for every path that ends an attempt: effects left STARTED unobserved are UNKNOWN in the ending's transaction, CANCELLED first, else UNKNOWN_EFFECT; the runner's end reads the effects as a reconcile does, not only the executor's word, through the function the cancel's pass uses too.

PR #34's CI failed from round 3 on: not M2-T07's code but the product job's `cargo deny --locked check`, which since then reports RUSTSEC-2026-0459 (CVE-2026-119375) for the bundled SQLite of `libsqlite3-sys 0.38.2` on every branch; the local self-test's deny ran licenses, bans and sources only. Fixed on main by PR #35 (rusqlite =0.41.0, libsqlite3-sys 0.39.1, SQLite 3.54.1), reviewed on its own; from now on the self-test runs `cargo deny --locked check` whole.

- Codex: `codex` codex-cli 0.166.0 (default model), session `01a1c236-1bf9-7922-a370-95192b674ac2`, 510 s; attempt record sha256 `66cb3095a479fef943e2746cf5930661f98ef002f9852969b524fb2324cabb65`, stdout sha256 `08cf456bc456fc2d25b5461e12bff341e019656c7851fc20219a6c0fd96eed01`.
- Claude: `claude` 2.1.319, model claude-opus-5-5, session `46647084-2209-405b-bde6-85909573bb9f`, 925 s; attempt record sha256 `88c7b8a01837b7a7a0ab34253bb8334aff7f5bb17f7203071da50b7ecc48c1d1`, stdout sha256 `f87a86c00dd49108ec7d15476c6455fdd41f5741035602942aea912a68e44361`.

Both reviewers found, each reproduced under a private manager, that HoH wrote and deleted paths it took from the manager's merged configuration: the instance's environment file named by `systemctl show` (with a drop-in adding a file, the last line, the user's, was overwritten) and its directory removed with `rm -rf` by the uninstall (a drop-in pointing it into a project's `.hoh/` or at `~/.config/hoh/` lost that data). Design correction 1 (ADR-023): everything HoH writes or deletes is under the installation's root R (`$XDG_DATA_HOME/hoh`, found from the binary's own `R/versions/<v>/bin/hoh`) or at the installer's fixed places; the manager's view is only checked against it. Both agreed with the developer's reading of the surviving mutant `client-unit-ready-without-its-mainpid` as equivalent (Claude explicitly).

|---|---|---|---|---|

Test: a drop-in pointing the file into the project's `.hoh/` with a `state.db` beside it: both unchanged after the uninstall. |

Tests: a drop-in adding a file (the host gets both, the user's file unchanged); a drop-in replacing the file (the install's `--project` 69, the client's host not the unit's with the reason, the user's file unchanged, nothing under `R/hosts`). |

- `20c6067`'s push run failed TEST-041-02 with "nothing in cancels.term". The stuck agent's TERM trap forked `date`; the shell had made the file before the exec, and the SIGKILL after the 300 ms term grace could end a slow `date` before it wrote. Reproduced every time with a 0.5 s `date` on PATH. Corrected in `c988dae`: a builtin write, its time the file's mtime; green with the slow `date`. The recovery binary has run TEST-005-04 beside it since this task, which adds load.
- `fa237be`'s pull_request run failed `identity::tests::a_process_whose_command_name_is_not_utf8_has_an_identity_and_a_group` with "/proc/7026/stat has no process group". This is M2T07R-1100's error, from the scan of every process' stat, and has not been reproduced. Its follow-up (the error naming the bytes read) is brought forward in `5470c5a`. No retry was added: its cause is still unproven, and the follow-up stays open before M2-T11.

Both judge the protocol closed as a whole:
- between the uninstall and every kind of run (client, `__host`, `__gate`, `__unit`, the WAL self-test), across reinstalls and upgrades;
- between installers of one installation;
- with no cycle between the two locks.

Codex checked every run refuses while the run lock is held exclusive, and that an old version's `__gate` survives an upgrade and keeps the uninstall from removing anything until it ends. Both confirm round 4's findings closed. The remaining findings were mostly about the installers' lock on `$HOME`, and are fixed by design correction 7 (the lock moved to its own file `$XDG_DATA_HOME/hoh.lock`). ADR-023 now also states the current protocol in one section (-457).

Both found a run lock's failure said as a running program (-501, -551): a lock failure said as contention a third time (-401, -453). Design correction 8 of ADR-023: an installer that will change anything holds the installers' lock, with no exception, and only an uninstall that finds nothing (and so changes nothing) takes none; every lock's result is told by flock's conflict code alone, in install.sh through one function and in the run through `unit::lock_refusal`.

Their common cause: each observation the removal rests on had two answers, and "cannot tell" went to one of them. Design correction 11 of ADR-023: every such observation has three (yes, no, cannot tell), and "cannot tell" refuses; no value a decision rests on comes from a form that loses failures (a process substitution feeding a loop, a command substitution as an argument, `|| true`); hoh's codes are passed on only when they are install.sh's own (64, 69, 78).

Both confirm round 9's findings closed by their dispositions. Correction 11's rule (cannot tell refuses) was not carried through everywhere: Codex found an `is-enabled` answer taken by its text alone (-900: "disabled" with exit 23) and "masked" taken for not enabled (-901), and bash's file tests answering "no" for a path they could not look at (-902: a unit directory under one that cannot be searched; -903: the command link in a `~/.local/bin` that cannot be searched); and a test that leaked its temporary root (-904). Claude found correction 11's own `find -L` refusing on any link not HoH's whose target cannot be resolved (-950), and five smaller gaps. Design correction 12 of ADR-023: existence has three answers too (`there`: a path is not there only when the nearest ancestor that is there can be searched); a manager's answer is taken only with the exit code its state gives, and "masked" cannot be told; the enabling links are listed where systemd reads them, one level down, by name, no target looked at, and the installer uses no find.

Design correction 13 of ADR-023, written before the code: the installer follows links itself, one at a time (`readlink`, a relative target against the link's directory, at most 40 like the kernel, beyond which nothing reaches through them), and says "not there" only from a sight; the way to a path is judged the same (`way`); the scan reads only `.wants`, `.requires` and `.upholds` directories, names beginning with a dot included, and one `-d` does not see is asked of `there`.

Its rerun failed in another test, unrelated to this task: hoh-agents' `claude_runner_mode_cancel_is_one_sigint_and_nothing_after_it` found its stand-in's signal record empty after 3 s; kept as a follow-up with the earlier unexplained ones (-854, -900, M2T07R-1100), not loosened.

Both confirm round 12's findings closed for their stated cases. New: a start job a client left in the window after its deadline went on past the uninstall's final pass, which took an instance that was not enabled for settled (-1200, high: the run lock covers runs, not the jobs they leave in the manager); the scan's `|| true` took a process of this user whose program it could not read for an ended one (-1201); any rmdir failure was said as foreign files with exit 0 (-1202); a regular file named as an instance in a .wants directory was taken for an enabling link (-1203); `readlink -f` failing once the root's link target and its parent were gone, the class of -1150 a second time (-1251); `sha256sum` escaping a file name with a newline in the version's name (-1250); text (-1252). Design correction 15 of ADR-023: an instance is stopped only when inactive or failed with no job pending, and the final pass acts on any not both not enabled and stopped; a program path the scan cannot read is excused only for a process gone, not this user's, denied by the kernel, or ended; rmdir's failure is said and ends with 74; only symbolic links enable; the scan resolves the root with `readlink -m`, and the boundary that remains is written; the version is named from the binary's content.

The new findings are all in correction 15's scan rule and words: a process exiting during the scan answers "No such file or directory" and was taken for one that cannot be told (-1350; one stuck exiting would refuse every uninstall); under /proc's hidepid=1 another user's process answers "Operation not permitted", not "Permission denied" (-1351, from the kernel's source; not verifiable here); the final pass said "started again" when it had only failed to ask (-1352); text (-1353); two behaviours untested (-1354). Design correction 16 of ADR-023: the scan judges an unreadable program path by the kernel's reason only, one look with `readlink -v` in the C locale ("No such file or directory": no address space; "Permission denied" or "Operation not permitted": denied a look; any other reason cannot be told, said with the kernel's words); the final pass says what it saw.

## M2T07R-1100 follow-up: a process being released is in no process group (PR #38, branch `task/M2T07R-1100-released-group`)

M2T07R-1100 (M2-T07 round 13, medium, deferred before M2-T11): `identity::group_left`, the /proc scan used for diagnostics and test evidence only, failed with "…/stat has no process group". Its cause, seen once with the bytes read (the diagnostic of 5470c5a, in M2-T14 candidate m2t14-009's unit check: `"306724 (sleep) X 0 -1 -1 …"`): the field is there and is `-1`. The kernel's `do_task_stat` (fs/proc/array.c) starts `pgid` at -1 and sets it only once `lock_task_sighand` succeeds; `__exit_signal` (kernel/exit.c) detaches the task from its group (`__unhash_process`, `detach_pid(PIDTYPE_PGID)`) before it clears `tsk->sighand`, so a stat showing -1 is of a task in no group. The two earlier failures, M2-T07 round 13 (`52b3472`, before 5470c5a) and `fa237be`'s CI run (M2-T14 round 1), carried no bytes; their cause is the same by elimination, not seen (M2T07R-1201). The records' "a stat with no group field" (M2-T07 round 13, PROGRESS) and the plan "skip a process in state X" (M2-T14 round 8) are corrected here (M2T07R-1202): the state is read before the lock (array.c), so a reaping task can show `Z … -1 -1`, and an `X` task before `__exit_signal` still shows its real group; the rule is the value, not the state.

The fix, `93c95b8` (prepared on the M2-T14 branch, its identity.rs blob the same) cherry-picked onto main 97fdbfa as `73a5a5f`: the pgrp field is read as the `pid_t` it is; -1 and 0 are no member of the (positive) group scanned; a value below -1 or past i32 stays an error carrying what was read (M2T05R-200). 0 is a group not visible in the /proc mount's pid namespace, and also kernel threads' pgrp, and was accepted before; values from pid_max up to i32::MAX are accepted as before, as no member of a group named by a real pgid (M2T07R-1201: the commit message's "anything else the kernel does not print stays an error" overstates it; the pushed message stays, the PR body is corrected). Test `identity::tests::a_process_already_released_is_in_no_group`: red on `936312d` and on `97fdbfa` (identity.rs identical in both) with the seen error. Mutants `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t07r1100-fix-mutations/` 6 of 6 on `93c95b8` and on `73a5a5f`. Self-test of `73a5a5f`: fmt, clippy, workspace 971 passed / 0 failed, cargo deny, bundle 0.

Candidate m2t07r1100-001 (`73a5a5f`, tree `fdc3607`, `CAND-fb084cb4…`): unit, integration, static, recovery, tui-pty and deployment exit 0 with the source unchanged (1021 passed, 0 failed; TEST-018-01..05, TEST-027-01..03, TEST-005-04, TEST-005-06 PASS). PR #38's CI on `73a5a5f`: pull_request green; the push run (42801472092) failed once in a pre-existing hoh-core test unrelated to identity.rs (`deliveries_taken_and_never_settled_stop_at_the_limit_and_cost_no_other_message`: each dispatcher tick runs under a 200 ms wall-clock timeout, which a slow runner spends before the fourth tick reaches its second message), kept with the CI follow-up, and its rerun (attempt 2) green in every job.

Review: two fresh sessions of two agents, both PASS.
- Codex: `codex` codex-cli 0.166.0 (default model), session `01a1c50e-f02c-7111-9708-c026eb4507cd`, 575 s; attempt record sha256 `f3988f3215b052fde1289c23815fd619ad5cd6c0d1e268fcf87697095c04a9f2`, stdout sha256 `882577180a0750a321ce1a431e40ecc957744ed6c0826fd8cd37e3ed4bdd9e2a`. The kernel facts checked against Linux 6.8, 7.0 and master; identity 22, process_substrate 13, probe 61 and the round-13 recovery test passed in a copy; every other stat parser checked.
- Claude: `claude` 2.1.319, model claude-opus-5-5, session `2d627f13-b67a-4781-aacd-2ec806fec513`, 555 s; attempt record sha256 `73324dac7565ea743360562e932714ec3614f0f4eeaa395d245c5c666c9c708f`, stdout sha256 `36d539e05fe53ef774638bc2bd25c4b1822f1b84f9ca775e8e800ca099d8559e`. Red-first reproduced; kernel lines cited; the records checked.

## CI reliability and test evidence (PR #39, branch `task/ci-reliability`)

Five pre-existing timing-sensitive tests failed in CI on 2026-11-10 (runs 42779925036, 42775489697 attempt 1, 42783826001, 42801472092 and 42804759906), and four test-evidence items were deferred here (M2T07R-1150, -1151 medium; -1200, -1203 low). The diagnosis (`.hoh-dev/evidence/ci-reliability-prep/README.md`) found one class: tests that take a transient or failed look at a process, a lock or a future for an answer. The design correction comes first, ADR-022 decision 10, convention v2 (`df5a62e`): a process has ended only per `identity::ended`; a group is empty only per the kernel's look-up; a child's state change is read only after an event the child makes; a lock a test relies on being free is let go explicitly (`LOCK_UN`); a future is cancelled only at a point known to be reached. Then one commit per item (`f115739` to `65b63ea`), each red first where an edit can make the old flaw certain (mutant set `.hoh-dev/evidence/ci-reliability-mutations/`, 9 of 9 on `65b63ea`). The claude SIGINT failure (42775489697) was observed only as its symptom, a signal record still empty 3 s after the cancel; its cause is unproven (the hypothesis: bash lost the INT in a gap of its trap handling), and the fix removes every gap a signal could be lost in rather than the hypothesised one.

Self-test of `65b63ea`: fmt, clippy, workspace 975 passed / 0 failed, cargo deny, bundle 0. Candidate ci-reliability-001 (`65b63ea`, tree `83827f3`, `CAND-a4916662…`): unit, integration, static, recovery, tui-pty and deployment exit 0 with the source unchanged (1025 passed, 0 failed; TEST-005-06 SKIPPED in integration, which needs a bundle, and PASS in deployment). CI on `65b63ea` green on the first try (push 42813076451, pull_request 42813086916).

The sweep (CIR-051, docs/19's complete invariant review): every `read_dir("/proc")` in the workspace (12; the five that took a failed listing for "none" converted, `installed::kill_strays` cleanup only), every `/proc` path's `exists()` (none left), every short timeout in a test (one: `a_claim_whose_dispatcher_stopped_waiting_costs_no_delivery`, the exception ADR-022 v2 now names: its lock is let go only after the cancel, so it cannot fail falsely, while its 150 ms cancel point remains a guess the product gives no event for), every signal-recording stand-in (five: the claude SIGINT and `stubborn` stand-ins and product's CANCEL turn now block the signals, say they are ready and take them with `sigtimedwait`, writing each record themselves; recovery's `StuckExecutor` says when its SIGTERM trap is set and the two tests that read its mark wait for it; control.rs's member already marked its readiness after its trap with a builtin), and every scan by command line (attach's now skips a copy forked and not yet exec'd, whose parent is a match: seen as two "hosts" in a local run of test_005_02 while these fixes were tested, red-first test `a_copy_forked_before_its_exec_is_no_second_process_of_its_command_line`).

Fix commit `2f4a1d8` (built in the detached worktree `ci-reliability-b`, then fast-forwarded). ### Candidate ci-reliability-002 and round 2 (two fresh sessions of two agents)

Candidate ci-reliability-002 (`08e9992`, tree `62fcd58`, `CAND-095372b7…`): unit, static, recovery, tui-pty and deployment exit 0 with the source unchanged; **integration exit 101**: `a_burst_of_keys_is_taken_in_full` and `a_sequence_split_by_a_slow_link_is_still_one_key` failed with "the alternate screen was not left", passing in the tui-pty check of the same candidate. Cause, from the code: `Session::ended` read the PTY's output right after hoh's exit, while the reader thread (its own thread, a 20 ms poll, no EIO while the test's slave stays open) could still have hoh's last bytes unread. Reproduced beside 24 busy loops: 2 of 20 runs of four TUI tests failed on `08e9992`, 0 of 20 with `Pty::drained`, which waits until a poll that started after the call found nothing to read (`.hoh-dev/evidence/ci-reliability-tui-drain/`); fixed in `f0bcf8b`. CI on `08e9992` green on the first try (push 42821853426, pull_request 42821864534).

Verified first (`.hoh-dev/evidence/ci-reliability-r3-kernel/`, raw output kept): the pidfd was readable early 9 times in about 350 million polls, while the parent's `waitid(WNOWAIT)` never answered early in about 94 million looks; Linux's pidfd_poll reads without the tasklist lock, a parent's wait under it. So a test's own unreaped child is judged by `identity::child_ended` (the parent's view, leaving it unreaped), a child the code under test reaps by `identity::reaped_held` (the held pidfd's fdinfo), and `identity::ended` stays for processes that are no children and exec, if at all, from their main thread, every such subject in the repository, its limit stated in the code and ADR-022. The thread-exec test now asserts the parent's view. |
| CIR-201 | medium | Codex | process.rs's two tests had moved from `!reaped(pid)` to `!exited(&held)`, accepting a zombie the supervisor left unreaped. | Fixed: `!reaped_held(&held, pid)` (reaped, through the pidfd held since the spawn). Mutant pair: a supervisor whose reap only looks (WNOWAIT) is killed by the new check and passes round 2's. |
| CIR-202 | low | Codex | tui_pty's `alive` took any failed look for "not alive", so the final `!alive` could pass on an error. | Fixed with -250. |

| CIR-251 | low | Claude | The deferred list missed more pre-existing signals by number (control.rs `Leaders`, retry.rs `Scripted`, recovery's two cancels, tui_pty test_043_03, pi_adapter, probe.rs). | The list completed: 18 sites, deferred (low) before M2-T11, named in ADR-022. |
| CIR-252 | low | Claude | `cancel_with_a_writer_outside` never required the writer to have run, so a missed trap passed; the ADR said such stand-ins could only fail. | Fixed: the writer must have written; the ADR states that a behaviour trap's behaviour must be asserted. Mutant: a trap that starts no writer is killed. |
| CIR-253 | low | Claude | Two TUI absence checks read after a fixed 300 ms sleep. | Fixed: a marker typed after the key is awaited first (keys are taken in order); the ADR names absence reads. |
| CIR-254 | low | Claude | The kernel and drain records kept no raw outputs. | The kernel scripts rerun with their outputs kept; round 2's README corrected: its "Z with 0 threads while the process ran on" was the last image's own zombie before its reap, not a transient, and the C stand-in's exec of /proc/self/exe could fail once its main thread had gone. The drain runs' raw outputs were not kept; its record says so. |

Mutants `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t17-mutations/` (13): on `37e1a26` 10 killed, `no-spec-applies-anyway` not compiling (its `-v2` killed by hand), `stale-notice-delivered` surviving; on `80ee1b5` 12 of 12 killed (`apply-80ee1b5.txt`).

## M2-T08 design reviews (ADR-025, Commander supervision)

The design went first, reviewed before most of the code: each round two fresh sessions of two agents (Codex, Claude) read a frozen worktree of the branch, with disjoint finding-id ranges (`M2T08D-<round*100>` for Codex, `+50` for Claude). Rounds 1 to 4 reviewed whole versions; from round 5 on, each round reviewed the fix diff of the version before, its call chain and its regressions. Raw outputs: `.hoh-dev/attempts/review-m2t08-d{,2..11}-{codex,claude}/`.

| Round | Reviewed | Codex | Claude | High |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `b412b42` (first version) | FAIL: -01..-16 (11 high, 4 medium, 1 low) | FAIL: -50..-67 (3 high, 8 medium, 7 low) | 14 |
| 2 | `8cd84f3` (second) | FAIL: -100..-112 (8 high) | FAIL: -150..-167 (3 high) | 11 |
| 3 | `b4e0e61` (third; CR-015 withdrawn) | FAIL: -200..-208 (4 high) | FAIL: -250..-269 (3 high) | 7 |
| 4 | `9d8c10a` (fourth) | FAIL: -300..-309 (4 high) | FAIL: -350..-363 (3 high) | 7 |
| 5 | `e8724dc` (fifth, a design correction) | FAIL: -400..-402 (1 high) | FAIL: -450..-460 (1 high) | 2 |
| 6 | `515555a` | FAIL: -500 (1 high) | PASS: -550..-558 (3 medium, 6 low) | 1 |
| 7 | `1e2e657` | FAIL: -600, -601 (2 high) | FAIL: -650..-655 (1 high, same as -601) | 2 |
| 8 | `dbdd49c` | FAIL: -700, -701 (1 high) | FAIL: -750..-755 (1 high) | 2 |
| 9 | `fca001c` | FAIL: -800..-802 (1 high) | FAIL: -850..-855 (1 high, same as -800) | 2 |
| 10 | `541f464`..`966feac` | FAIL: -900 (high, S2 code: a capped step fired before it caught up), -901 | FAIL: -950 (high, failures counted per run), -951..-955 | 2 |
| 11 | `382f2a2` (eleventh) | PASS: -1000 (medium) | PASS: -1054 (medium), -1050..-1053, -1055..-1057 (low) | 0 |

Each version's header in ADR-025 says which findings it answers and how; the fifth version was a design correction: the class "a rejected answer re-triggers itself, bounded by caps and a hand-over to a person" had come back four times (-106, -157, -267, -350; -302, -351 alike), so it went back to the requirement's text (only stale decisions re-trigger, REQ-021 behaviour 6). CR-015 (run revision for AC-021-04) was proposed in round 1 and withdrawn in round 2 as a loosening; the task-snapshot fence took its place.

Round 11 (two fresh sessions on `/home/ai/dev/hoh-wt/m2t08-design11` at `382f2a2`, the fix diff `86bf272..382f2a2`): Codex codex-cli 0.166.0, session `01a1c9d8-1745-7eb0-9b35-6490ada069a9`, 787 s, attempt record sha256 `1bfb61630cbc877b33671aaabf1e5fc14ce18060288c986c354a059e7d81cf17`; Claude 2.1.319 (claude-opus-5-5), session `e21c1210-c4e3-41d0-b0c0-5a4473b4a760`, 671 s, attempt record sha256 `9e2b0cf946ed105f6f09ee47bf8a2eb59205c43369d4db9baedf298cf50e19e5`. Both PASS; Codex ran `Store::supervise` on two issues named `fp#retrigger` on unknown revisions 9 and 10 and saw them merged (-1000); Claude replayed the S2 red tests on `86bf272` (12 passed, 3 failed for their reasons) and the fix on `382f2a2` (15/15).

| Id | Severity | Agent | Finding | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T08D-1000 | medium | Codex | The -954 fix recognised a re-trigger by its fingerprint's `#retrigger` suffix: a producer's fingerprint with that suffix lost its `@r<revision>` and two unknown revisions merged (REQ-021 behaviour 3). | Fixed: `classify` marks a `supervisor.retrigger` wake; only that one skips `@r` (ADR-025 v12 decision 2); regression test `a_producers_fingerprint_ending_like_a_retrigger_still_keys_by_its_unknown_revision`. |
| M2T08D-1052 | low | Claude | Same as -1000. | Fixed with -1000. |
| M2T08D-1054 | medium | Claude | With failures counted per wake, the recovery path was unstated: how a new Commander epoch zeroes counts, which wake the half-open probe takes, whether preempted/withdrawn rearms break "consecutive". | Fixed in ADR-025 v12 decisions 3 and 5: failures are those of the current Commander epoch (`supervisor.rearmed` records the epoch); preempted/withdrawn neither count nor break the run; the probe takes the oldest capped wake, the next capped one may be probed at once after a success, and the supervisor's claims are released only when no wake is capped. Implemented and tested with the unavailability in S4 (a new epoch allocates at once; the probe picks the capped wake). |
| M2T08D-1053 | low | Claude | Same-key merge took only unallocated wakes, so a rearmed wake and a new one each cost a turn; merged severity and the integrity edge unstated; "only one turn" overstated. | Fixed in v12 decision 3: merge into any same-key pending wake not in flight; severity is the maximum; a merge that makes a wake integrity is its edge; the wording says "the one in flight plus one more". |
| M2T08D-1055 | low | Claude | The no-starvation argument left out failure rearms and half-open probes; "at most three turns" does not hold with failures. | Fixed in v12 decision 3: both added, the sentence removed, the conclusion limited to a non-preemptable turn (not SETTLED) while the run stays admitted and the Commander active; S4's bounded test includes them. |
| M2T08D-1056 | low | Claude | The edge preemption was "in the same step", but a step is several transactions: a crash between `woken` and `preempt` loses the edge. | Fixed in v12 decision 3: the preempt is written in the transaction that writes `supervisor.woken` (or the merge that makes it integrity); S7 adds the crash point. |
| M2T08D-1051 | low | Claude | Catching up runs every batch in one writer job, so the urgent lane (cancel, pause) waits behind a long first step. | Deferred with a stated trade-off (v12 decision 2): normal steps take in only what came since the last; S7 measures the per-batch time and records the expected backlog; split the batches into their own jobs if it threatens AC-043-02. |
| M2T08D-1057 | low | Claude | The Commander directory still depends on the environment (shell vs unit), the "temp dir cleared" wording is stale, and adapter tests must not write the real `~/.local/state`. | Fixed in v12 decision 3: the first use records the absolute path (`commander.workdir`), later turns use it; tests point `XDG_STATE_HOME` and `HOME` at temp dirs. Implemented in S6. |
| M2T08D-1050 | low | Claude | Stale text after -900: the `STEP` and `step` comments and the S2 slice still said "per step". | Fixed in the comments and the slice list. |

Convergence: round 11 has no critical or high finding; the design stands at ADR-025 v12. The mediums and lows are fixed in text (and -1000 in code with a red test) and come back to the reviewers with the implementation's review, which also checks S3 onwards. Owner question recorded from round 9 (-802, -854), still open: the Commander runs with the spec's full-access profile (yolo-vm) and can write the writer's workspace by absolute path; making the Commander read-only would change the profile, which is the owner's call. It does not block the implementation.

## M2-T08 implementation reviews (PR #42, branch `task/M2-T08-supervision`)

Candidate m2t08-001 (`7caac2b`, tree `6947b08`, `CAND-eba64c43…`): self-test of `7caac2b` 1104 passed / 0 failed, fmt, clippy, deny, static, bundle 0 (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-selftest-1/`); the six checks unit, integration, static, recovery, tui-pty and deployment exit 0 with the source unchanged (TEST-021-01..04, TEST-019-02, TEST-043-04 PASS; TEST-005-06 SKIPPED in integration, which needs a bundle). CI on `7caac2b` green (PR #42: product, deployment, bundle ×4, push and pull_request runs). Reviewed diff `b90aa0c..7caac2b` (35 commits, 56 files), two fresh sessions of two agents with disjoint finding-id ranges (`M2T08R-01..49` Codex, `-50..99` Claude). Raw outputs: `.hoh-dev/attempts/review-m2t08-r1-{codex,claude}/`.

### Round 1 (two fresh sessions of two agents)

- Codex: `codex` codex-cli 0.166.0 (default model), session `01a1ca5f-e023-7f31-b56a-459ec49ed6f3`, 1003 s; attempt record sha256 `5624e6acefc304725d26b3617567466444f53e6ee282a6afebb5fc06dcad4183`, stdout sha256 `318e129b171125e85e30de33850a1c12c6c2f874e3600fa322568884f721f952`. FAIL (3 high, 5 medium): 833 source files' hashes checked against the candidate; core 312 and app 89 tests rerun in an archive copy; seven counterexample tests against the real store with a fake clock, an ingestion measurement (debug build: 1 000 / 2 000 / 4 000 / 8 000 / 16 000 same-key events in 138 / 415 / 1 532 / 5 701 / 22 645 ms) and a priority-lane CancelRun accepted after 22.6 s behind one supervisor job.
- Claude: `claude` 2.1.319, model claude-opus-5-5, session `0125076b-07b3-40ff-8fd1-7a5bfa5b9c53`, 1077 s; attempt record sha256 `eca29ac9a64cee99d7ab0b75e9a56fece988cf6990a5dfda7fd5fc7f0f963245`, stdout sha256 `3830576971b539ef5faaa12acaa17e3d0610a738683c14aa5b7bcd91634938de`. FAIL (2 high, 1 medium, 5 low): ADR-025 v12 checked decision by decision; four experiments appended to an archive copy's tests (a probe in flight, two failing Auth probes, a rearm after a handover, an ended run's owed wake).

| ID | Severity | Found by | Summary | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T08R-01 | high | Codex | The cap counted owed wakes only: with the half-open probe allocated, the next step saw no capped wake and released the supervisor's claims (BLOCKED rev 2 to RUNNING rev 3) before any recovery fact. | Fixed (design correction first, ADR-025 v13 `2618ae3`): one verdict per step; a wake not answered (owed or its probe in flight) with MAX_RETRIES failures is capped. Test `a_probe_in_flight_keeps_its_run_blocked_until_it_settles`. |
| M2T08R-50 | high | Claude | Same as -01 (owed() excludes the in-flight wake; the run flapped BLOCKED/RUNNING each cooldown). | Fixed with -01; obligations now include wakes in flight (decision 3). |
| M2T08R-51 | high | Claude | `supervisor.blocked`'s id followed the unavailable/available count; the -50 flap under unchanged reasons repeated it (UNIQUE failure), and the host tick aborted at that run's step before allocation and dispatches: no run of the project dispatched. | Fixed at the cause: the flap is gone (-50), so Auth probes keep one blocking episode (`an_auth_unavailable_commander_probed_again_and_again_keeps_its_run_blocked`); the id is numbered by the run's earlier blocking episodes (defensive: no remaining path repeats the old id, see the mutants below; `a_second_blocking_episode_under_the_same_reasons_is_its_own` checks a release and a second episode); a run's failing step is isolated in the host tick, said once on stderr, while a writer stopped, busy or full still ends the tick (host test `a_run_whose_supervisor_step_fails_holds_up_no_other_run`). |
| M2T08R-02 | high | Codex | `request_user_decision` claimed NEED_DECISION before decide: an epoch-1 answer after a handover to epoch 2 blocked the run with no decisions row (no STALE_ROLE_EPOCH). | Fixed (v13 decisions 6, 8): request_user_decision and create_tasks go through decide's fences; past them their decisions row stays PROPOSED (replay and KEY_REUSE by that row) and the request claims; BLOCKED and WAITING_EXTERNAL judge a request for a person. Tests `a_stale_request_for_a_person_is_rejected_and_retriggered_and_claims_nothing`, `a_blocked_or_waiting_run_judges_a_request_for_a_person`. |
| M2T08R-03 | high | Codex | Same-key ingestion rewrote the whole seq array per event (O(N²)), and all batches ran in one writer job, so a priority-lane cancel waited 22.6 s behind 16 000 events. | Fixed (v13 decision 2, replacing the -1051 trade-off): a batch joins its events per key in memory, one read and one write per key; each batch is one writer job and `Store::supervise` submits the next, so the priority lane waits one batch at most. Tests `a_batch_writes_each_keys_trigger_once_however_many_events_it_joins` (rows changed per batch), `a_step_is_one_writer_job_per_batch_until_it_has_caught_up`. Measured on `a1110ac` (debug build, `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r1-measure/`): 1 000 / 2 000 / 4 000 / 8 000 / 16 000 / 100 000 same-key events in 6 / 10 / 14 / 27 / 58 / 587 ms; a CancelRun submitted 20 ms into a 100 000-event catch-up was accepted after 30 ms (the step took 588 ms). |
| M2T08R-04 | medium | Codex | A run with no spec was still judged: NO_SPEC deferred, then BLOCKED(COMMANDER_UNAVAILABLE, NEED_DECISION). | Fixed (v13 decision 4): no verdict and no claims for a run with no spec. Test `a_run_with_no_spec_is_not_judged_whatever_its_commander`. |
| M2T08R-05 | medium | Codex | Firing came before the judgement: a routine trigger found due late became a wake, then an obligation that blocked the run. | Fixed (v13 decision 5): the verdict comes first; a due routine trigger is superseded while the Commander is unavailable. Test `an_overdue_routine_trigger_is_superseded_not_woken_while_the_commander_is_unavailable`. |
| M2T08R-06 | medium | Codex | An ended run was stepped only with a PENDING trigger, so an owed wake (or an output) of a run that ended without CANCEL_REQUESTED was never superseded. | Fixed (v13 decision 3): an ended run is stepped while it has a woken or an output since its last `supervisor.closed`; the step supersedes what is left and writes `supervisor.closed` once nothing is. Test `an_ended_runs_owed_wake_is_stepped_superseded_and_closed`. |
| M2T08R-54 | low | Claude | Same as -06. | Fixed with -06. |
| M2T08R-07 | medium | Codex | The stored key told a re-trigger by a suffix an ordinary fingerprint can carry: `fp#retrigger` on a known revision joined the re-trigger of `fp` (and its own-mistake backoff). | Fixed (v13 decision 2): an event's own fingerprint has `%`, `#`, `@` escaped, so only the Runtime's suffixes carry `#` and `@`. Tests `an_ordinary_fingerprint_is_escaped_so_only_the_runtimes_suffixes_carry_hash_and_at`, `an_ordinary_fingerprint_never_joins_a_retrigger_chains_key`. |
| M2T08R-08 | medium | Codex | The preempt's condition used a reduced admission without the registry: a Commander the registry found unavailable still had its turn preempted. | Fixed (v13 decisions 3, 5): `could_allocate` takes the step's verdict; unavailable, no preempt. Test `an_unavailable_commander_is_not_preempted_for`. |
| M2T08R-52 | medium | Claude | TEST-021-03's clauses were vacuous: only token.delta up to the cursor, no Commander output to replay. | Fixed: the fixture puts need_decision events of the same key (seq 100) and another key (110) and an output its decision ended (115, 116) below the cursor; the test asserts 100 not joined, no trigger for 110, the output not disposed of again. Red through mutants (cursor ignored; ended output re-disposed). |
| M2T08R-53 | low | Claude | A failure rearmed after a handover was counted under the binding's new epoch. | Fixed (v13 decision 3): recorded under the epoch of the attempt that failed. Test `a_failure_rearmed_after_a_handover_counts_under_its_turns_epoch`. |
| M2T08R-55 | low | Claude | TEST-021-04's first STALE_REVISION comes from the run revision's CAS, so the task-snapshot fence could be removed with the test passing; "task stays 8, no dispatch" is vacuous for a record_assumption. | Deferred (low): AC-021-04's scenario moves the run and the task together by design (ADR-025 decision 6); the fence alone is covered by `a_decision_on_a_changed_task_is_stale_and_retriggered_once_with_the_new_revision`. |
| M2T08R-56 | low | Claude | The promised crash-point test (woken and preempt in one commit) was missing, and so was the -1051 measurement. | Fixed: `an_integrity_edges_woken_and_its_preempt_are_one_commit` fails the step's first commit (neither written) and its second (both already in the first); a mutant writing the preempt in a second commit is killed. The measurement is in -03's row. |
| M2T08R-57 | low | Claude | The summary bounded events and rejections but not tasks or other wakes, and had no causal-chain step. | Fixed in part: at most 50 tasks and 50 other wakes with their totals, snapshots never cut (test `a_turns_summary_bounds_its_tasks_and_other_wakes_and_keeps_every_snapshot`). The causal-chain order is deferred to M2-T09's context packer (its ordering decision), stated in v13 decision 7. |

Design correction before the fixes: five findings of one class (the Commander's availability seen through different views: -01/-50, -04, -05, -08, -51) answered by ADR-025 v13 (`2618ae3`): one verdict per step, computed before firing and used by firing, the preempt's condition, the claims and the allocation; obligations include wakes in flight. Fix commit `a1110ac` in the worktree `hoh-wt/m2t08-fix` (branch `task/M2-T08-fix` from `7caac2b`; the reviewed worktree untouched while the reviews ran). Red first: `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r1/red.txt` (13 of 14 new tests failed for their reasons; the crash-point test passed by construction and is red through its mutant). Existing tests changed by the correction are listed in ADR-025's appendix. Self-test of `a1110ac`: fmt, clippy, workspace 1121 passed / 0 failed, deny, bundle 0 (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r1-selftest/` in the fix worktree). Mutants `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r1-fix-mutations/` (one per fix, each reverting it): 20 of 21 killed on `a1110ac` (`apply-a1110ac.txt`); the survivor numbers `supervisor.blocked` as `7caac2b` did, which repeats only for a third blocking episode under one unavailability record; after -50's fix no path reaches it (NOTES.md: claims are released under an unchanged verdict only when a turn allocated before the unavailability settles), so the episode numbering is defensive and the mutant equivalent in practice.

### Candidate m2t08-002 and round 2 (the round-1 fixes; two fresh sessions of two agents)

Candidate m2t08-002 (`3121803`, tree `740c45a`, `CAND-cbcc6c39…`): the six checks exit 0 with the source unchanged (TEST-021-01..04, TEST-019-02, TEST-043-04 PASS; TEST-005-06 SKIPPED in integration, PASS in deployment). CI on `3121803` green. Reviewed: the fix diff `7caac2b..3121803`.

- Codex: `codex` codex-cli 0.166.0 (default model), session `01a1ca93-5c17-7d83-977b-3f849790b5f0`, 763 s; attempt record sha256 `621e9289da124281aca6a1c387a63bdac54b39288b5baabb846bf6fd11d1bbfc`, stdout sha256 `106aa2c47dbe39f731738b8537dbfec0585d6d234a9ad1efd80ab654a9c5f933`. FAIL (2 high, 2 medium): four counterexamples in an archive copy against the real store; a cancel behind a due trigger of 8 000 / 16 000 / 32 000 / 100 000 distinct tasks accepted after 180 / 642 / 2 438 / 22 099 ms.
- Claude: `claude` 2.1.319, model claude-opus-5-5, session `22bb3678-2efa-490c-a481-0d3a27223f6e`, 1559 s; attempt record sha256 `08a03dd86d38c61dae6d5d1a518c5ba827d2a3ca3826704c1015ba6eaf0f4776`, stdout sha256 `8c8a08612b9d19ad4347896440ffaa05567a70e1d2c77f76b336dee74b865a12`. PASS (1 medium, 4 low): the workspace in an archive copy (1121 passed; `test_062_06` not run, its target on tmpfs); seven mutants of its own (three on the per-key aggregation, two on the host's isolation survived); four experiments.

| ID | Severity | Found by | Summary | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T08R-100 | high | Codex | Auth unavailability was read from the last ended turn's class: a probe failing for another reason counted as recovery (RUNNING, a decision-class task dispatched) with no SETTLED turn. | Fixed (design correction 2, ADR-025 v14 `bb4600e`): Auth and Capability hold until a later turn of the epoch settles (or the epoch changes); a probe failing otherwise only restarts the cooldown. Test `an_auth_unavailability_outlasts_a_probe_that_fails_for_another_reason`. |
| M2T08R-101 | high | Codex | Firing read a trigger's events one by one and deduplicated their tasks by a linear search: quadratic, in one writer job; a cancel waited 22 s behind 100 000 distinct tasks. | Fixed (v14 decision 2): one statement for a trigger's events, a set for its tasks, the merge found by key, `of_run` indexed by wake id, the summary reading only the shown events' payloads. Measured (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r2-measure/`, debug build, the reviewer's scenario): 8 000 / 32 000 / 100 000 tasks, cancel accepted after 187 / 2 374 / 22 579 ms on `3121803` and 17 / 129 / 461 ms after. No functional test can tell a set from a linear search; the measurement is the evidence. |
| M2T08R-102 | medium | Codex | Rearms were written only by the allocation, so in the host's order (step, then allocation) the step counted one failure fewer: right after the eighth failure a routine trigger was woken. | Fixed (v14 decisions 3, 5): the step folds the ended turns (the same `rearm`) before its verdict; the host gives the allocation the registry's looks too. Test `the_step_counts_an_ended_turns_failure_before_its_verdict` (host order). |
| M2T08R-103 | medium | Codex | The claims were released at a turn's SETTLED, before its outputs were disposed of; a stale output's re-trigger blocked again only at the next step. | Fixed (v14, narrowed by v15): a probe's wake is owed while the probe is in flight and, settled, until its outputs are disposed of. Test `a_probe_settled_while_its_commander_is_unavailable_otherwise_is_owed_until_its_outputs_are_disposed_of` (also a second blocking episode). For an ordinary turn the case disappears with -150. |
| M2T08R-150 | medium | Claude | v13 counted every wake in flight as blocking: a Commander becoming unavailable during an ordinary turn blocked the run (revision +1), the turn's valid answer was rejected as stale and re-triggered, and the run flipped back and forth; a regression from `7caac2b`, the same class as round 1. | Fixed (design correction 3, ADR-025 v15 `8523dae`, the class taken as a whole): blocking obligations are owed wakes and probe turns (in flight, or settled with outputs waiting); an ordinary turn, allocated while the Commander was available, is answering its wake and blocks nothing; decision 3's invariant states it. Test `a_turn_in_flight_when_its_commander_becomes_unavailable_is_answered_and_blocks_nothing` (the answer APPLIED, nothing re-triggered, a new decision then blocks). |
| M2T08R-151 | low | Claude | The per-key aggregation's due time (from the first and last commits) and severity maximum had no test (three mutants survived). | Fixed: `a_backlog_of_one_key_taken_in_by_one_batch_is_due_two_seconds_after_its_last_commit`, `an_integrity_event_joined_in_a_batch_makes_its_trigger_due_at_once`. |
| M2T08R-152 | low | Claude | The host's isolation clauses had no test and the failure was only on stderr: a run whose step always fails was invisible in `/v1/events`. | Fixed in part: `supervisor.step_failed{error}` once per run and error (v15 decision 5), asserted by the host test. Deferred (low): a test of the clause that still ends the tick on a stopped, busy or full writer (no fault hook reaches the supervisor's job in a host test; the clause is three error kinds in one match arm). |
| M2T08R-153 | low | Claude | ADR-011's note was stale after v13 (BLOCKED's list; the PROPOSED row of an unsupported decision against §3.6 and "Not adopted"). | Fixed (text): the note states the fences, the PROPOSED row, its readers' scope (`decide` direct calls write nothing; the dangling-row alternative is another case) and M2-T13's closing of it. Claude checked every reader of the decisions table: the PROPOSED rows are harmless. |
| M2T08R-154 | low | Claude | The causal-order handoff (-57) to M2-T09 was not in decision 10's list for PROGRESS. | Fixed (text): listed in decision 10; added to PROGRESS's follow-ups at merge. |

Three design corrections in one task for one class (v13, v14, v15): v13 unified who judges the Commander, v14 the facts it judges by (ended turns folded first, recovery only by its fact), v15 which turns are obligations (ordinary turns answer, probes owe). Fix commits `b493030` (v14 fixes) and `b1df0ab` (v15 fixes), in the fix worktree while the reviewed one stayed frozen. Red first: `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r2-red.txt` (the three v14 tests on `3121803`), `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r2b-red.txt` (-150's test on `b493030`; the probe test passed there, v14's rule being wider, and is red through its mutant). Self-tests: `b493030` workspace 1124 passed / 0 failed, clippy; `b1df0ab` fmt, clippy, workspace 1126 passed / 0 failed, deny, bundle 0 (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r2-selftest/`, `m2t08-r2b-selftest/`). Mutants `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r2-fix-mutations/` (one per round-2 fix): 9 of 9 killed on `b1df0ab`; round 1's set replayed there: 17 killed, its survivor as before, three patterns moved (r50's property held by r150, r103 and r01; r04 and the host's r51 retargeted, 2 of 2 killed). -101 has no functional mutant; its evidence is the measurement.

### Candidate m2t08-003 and round 3 (the round-2 fixes; two fresh sessions of two agents)

Candidate m2t08-003 (`cc73641`, tree `e6707f6`, `CAND-c4c2f37c…`): the six checks exit 0 with the source unchanged (TEST-021-01..04, TEST-019-02, TEST-043-04 PASS; TEST-005-06 SKIPPED in integration, PASS in deployment). CI on `cc73641` green. Reviewed: the fix diff `3121803..cc73641`.

- Codex: `codex` codex-cli 0.166.0 (default model), session `01a1cabc-a272-75c2-a4ab-1cb86d82eed8`, 798 s; attempt record sha256 `69d6490cfac2ad8c9082173199ab9357a4d548e2698f99a1f6931fa111789763`, stdout sha256 `400de3582e41972484acfb2094bf087bf21e8dee4512b6ab3521f999b4c3187b`. FAIL (2 high, 1 low): a real handover through ConfigureTeam, checkpoint, bind and session.start; output disposal measured behind a cancel of another run.
- Claude: `claude` 2.1.319, model claude-opus-5-5, session `356df3b2-2fe6-4a45-b7f2-ac06ba2b9d18`, 1594 s; attempt record sha256 `4af755b5b33a2596f2df36a1b1c425744a1ea0df2ca26298bc055ecd75bc35a5`, stdout sha256 `e92a44bda1f4e52df96aac8b0167c865272fd2685df76303a34205f4c0a6ec1a`. FAIL (1 high, 4 medium, 4 low): host-level experiments with a running host (a probe's answer between OBSERVED and SETTLED; a registry flip during an ordinary turn), its own mutants (seven), the workspace in an archive copy (1125 passed; `test_062_06` skipped, its target on tmpfs).

| ID | Severity | Found by | Summary | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T08R-200 | high | Codex | The registry's look count is per agent, but a recorded registry fault was compared with any agent's look: after a handover to another agent the new, healthy Commander stayed BLOCKED by the old agent's fault. | Fixed (design correction 4, ADR-025 v16 `596f1af`, decision 5's reasons table: each reason's subject, fact and recovery fact): the observation records its agent and applies only while that agent holds the role. Test `a_registry_fault_of_the_old_agent_does_not_hold_a_commander_handed_to_another`. The same class found by the developer's audit: inactivity was timed from an earlier holder's deferral; deferrals now carry their epoch (`a_commander_without_a_session_is_timed_from_its_own_epochs_deferral`). |
| M2T08R-201 | high | Codex | Output disposal looked up each output's end by a correlated scan and ran every output in one writer job: a cancel of another run waited 32.7 s behind 8 000 historical outputs and 108.7 s behind one turn's 1 000 invalid outputs. | Fixed (v16 decision 2's jobs table: every supervisor job bounded): the pending set is built once per job (one scan of the outputs, one of their ends), each turn's allocation read once, at most 50 outputs per writer job. Test `a_turns_outputs_are_disposed_of_at_most_fifty_per_writer_job`. Measured (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r3-measure/`, debug build): history of 1 000 / 4 000 / 8 000 outputs, cancel accepted after 323 / 5 540 / 25 160 ms on `cc73641` and 1 / 0 / 7 ms after; bursts of 250 / 500 / 1 000, 1 778 / 13 954 / 109 364 ms before and 0 / 1 / 1 ms after. |
| M2T08R-202 | low | Codex | `step_failed` compared with the last record only, so errors alternating A, B, A were written three times. | Fixed: once per run and error. Test `a_runs_failing_step_is_recorded_once_per_error_however_they_alternate`. |
| M2T08R-255 | low | Claude | Same as -202. | Fixed with -202. |
| M2T08R-250 | high | Claude | Outputs were recorded at OBSERVED and disposed of whatever their turn's state, while the SETTLED came in another writer job: a probe's answer was judged on the still-BLOCKED run, and a dispatch or transition_run answer was refused (ILLEGAL_TRANSITION) and lost. | Fixed (design correction 5, ADR-025 v17 `0412592`: every action only on facts the step's verdict saw): a turn's outputs are disposed of only once the step saw it end (its `attempt.moved` end at or below the step's cursor), a turn that ended otherwise has them discarded. Tests `a_probes_answer_waits_for_its_turn_to_settle_and_is_judged_on_the_working_run` (judged after the release: stale by construction and re-triggered once, never lost) and `a_disposal_job_leaves_the_outputs_of_a_turn_its_step_did_not_see_settle`. |
| M2T08R-251 | medium | Claude | The allocation recomputed availability at its own moment: a probe settling between the step and the allocation let an ordinary turn be allocated on the still-BLOCKED run, its answer then made stale by the release. | Fixed (v17 decision 5): the allocation acts only when the step's recorded verdict and its own view agree; otherwise nothing until the next step. Test `the_allocation_waits_for_the_step_when_its_facts_changed_since_the_verdict`. |
| M2T08R-252 | medium | Claude | A run blocking for another obligation while an ordinary turn is in flight (or its answer undisposed) still makes that answer stale, contrary to v15's text. | Fixed in text (v17 decision 3: the exception stated, one extra turn, nothing lost) with a test of it: `an_ordinary_answer_overtaken_by_its_run_blocking_for_another_decision_is_retriggered_once`. Blocking promptly for the other owed decision is what REQ-021 behaviour 8 asks; deferring it for the turn in flight would delay it up to a turn's limit. |
| M2T08R-253 | medium | Claude | -100's recovery rule was tested one way only (three mutants survived: a SETTLED turn not clearing Auth, no epoch filter, a withdrawn turn clearing it). | Fixed: `an_auth_unavailability_ends_by_a_settled_turn_and_not_by_a_withdrawn_one`, `an_auth_unavailability_ends_with_its_commanders_epoch`; the three mutants are in the round-3 set. |
| M2T08R-254 | medium | Claude | The replaced v14 test's property (a stale answer's re-trigger keeps the run BLOCKED at the next step) had no assertion left (a mutant dropping PENDING triggers from the obligations survived). | Fixed: `a_probes_stale_answer_keeps_its_run_blocked_through_the_retrigger`; that mutant is in the round-3 set. |
| M2T08R-256 | low | Claude | A cancelled run whose step always fails (an unreadable trigger) was never closed and stepped every tick. | Fixed: superseding a cancelled or ended run's triggers does not read their seqs (`a_cancelled_runs_unreadable_trigger_is_still_superseded`). |
| M2T08R-257 | low | Claude | v14's "the allocation is given the registry's looks" had no test (a mutant survived). | No change: the mutant is equivalent in practice. Without the looks, the allocation's registry reason falls back to the recorded look, which the step wrote from the same looks in the same tick; the wiring stays for one view. |
| (audit) | — | developer | Found while checking -250's fix: a supervision turn reconciled to UNKNOWN_EFFECT (its host died with the work begun) was never ended in the wakes model (only terminal states were), so its wake stayed in flight and the run's supervision stopped for good; ADR-025 decision 3 says such a turn ends and its wake is rearmed. | Fixed in code to match decision 3: UNKNOWN_EFFECT ends a supervision turn (a failure, rearmed, asked again; its outputs, if any, discarded). Test `a_turn_reconciled_to_an_unknown_effect_has_ended_and_its_wake_is_rearmed` (red on `ac53556`: no further turn). |
| M2T08R-258 | low | Claude | Round 1's r51 replay on `b1df0ab` ran no test (its test was replaced by v15), so "survives as before" was vacuous. | Corrected: retargeted to the probe test it still survives (Claude ran it: 1 passed), equivalent in practice as the round-1 NOTES say. |

Five design corrections in one task for this lifecycle (v13 to v17). v16 wrote the two recurring classes out whole as tables (unavailability reasons: subject, fact, recovery; supervisor jobs: each bounded); v17 closed the remaining gap between host writer jobs: every action acts only on facts the step's one verdict saw. Fix commits `e40d082` (v16), `ac53556` (v17) and `8e49a24` (the UNKNOWN_EFFECT end). Red first: `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r3-red.txt` (v16's three on `cc73641`), `m2t08-r3b-red.txt` (v17's on `e40d082`: -250, -251, -256 red; -252, -253, -254 test gaps, red through their mutants)., `m2t08-r3c-red.txt` (the UNKNOWN_EFFECT end on `ac53556`). Self-tests: `e40d082` 1130 passed / 0 failed; `ac53556` 1138 / 0; `8e49a24` fmt, clippy, workspace 1139 passed / 0 failed, deny, bundle 0 (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r3-selftest/`, `m2t08-r3b-selftest/`, `m2t08-r3c-selftest/`). Mutants `.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r3-fix-mutations/`: 12 of 12 killed on `ac53556` (one per fix, plus Claude's surviving m250, m251, m252 and m257); `r3c/` on `8e49a24`: the UNKNOWN_EFFECT end killed, round 1's r51 retargeted to the probe test survives (equivalent in practice, -258); round 2's set replayed on `ac53556`: 8 killed, r152's pattern moved (held by r202).

| ID | Severity | Found by | Summary | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| M2T08R-300 | high | Codex | The persisted registry fault was rewritten only when the reasons' names changed: handed to another agent unavailable for the same reason, the record still named the old agent, and a new host with no look yet judged the Commander available (RUNNING, an ordinary turn allocated). | Fixed (design correction 6, ADR-025 v18 `999c0fd`: the two tables item by item): a persisted fact names its subject and is rewritten when the subject changes. | M2T08R-301 | high | Codex | The 50-output bound did not bound the decision fence: each decision compared every task snapshot by its own query; 50 decisions over 100 000 snapshots made one 30 s job. | Fixed (v18 decision 2): the fence compares all snapshots in one statement; a bridged output is fenced by its own turn's allocation (read once per job, by its event id); disposal jobs are budgeted by snapshot units (`DISPOSE_BUDGET` 10 000, at least one output). Test `a_disposal_job_is_budgeted_by_the_snapshots_its_decisions_compare`. Measured (`.hoh-dev/evidence/m2t08-r4-measure/`): the whole step at 100 000 snapshots x 50 decisions 29.2 s (one job) on `8e49a24`, 10.0 s over ~50 jobs of ~0.2 s after. |
| M2T08R-302 | high | Codex | The NO_SPEC deferral check compared each trigger's id in JSON across the run's deferrals, quadratic and repeated each tick: a cancel waited 26 s at 8 000 triggers. | Fixed (v18 decision 2): found by its event id `<trigger>/deferred/NO_SPEC`. Measured: 1 000 / 4 000 / 8 000 triggers, cancel accepted after 353 / 5 688 / 26 056 ms before and 0 / 11 / 41 ms after. |
| M2T08R-350 | medium | Claude | -250's class in a narrower case: a probe settled while its Commander was unavailable for another reason (the registry) had its answer judged on the still-BLOCKED run; a dispatch or transition_run was refused (ILLEGAL_TRANSITION) and lost. | Fixed (design correction 7, ADR-025 v19 `13799c3`): a probe's outputs are disposed of only once the step's recorded verdict is available (claims released first; the answer stale by construction and re-triggered once). Test `a_probe_settled_while_its_commander_is_unavailable_otherwise_is_owed_until_its_outputs_are_disposed_of` (rewritten: its answer is now a dispatch that waits, then is re-triggered). |
